* System 1 from UIR2. X$Flow 1=exp, 2=hypoexp, 3=uniform, 4=trace.
* For UIR2 use X$Flow=1, X$Gap=1 and X$MeanS=2.
* For trace use X$Gap=169.573210 and X$MeanS=load*X$Gap/0.6.
BufferTwo STORAGE 4
WaitSmo   TABLE MP1,1,1,500
SojSmo    TABLE M1,1,1,500
IntExp    VARIABLE (Exponential(2,0,X$Gap))
IntHypo   VARIABLE (Exponential(2,0,X$Gap#107.337420/169.573210)+Exponential(2,0,X$Gap#31.117895/169.573210)+Exponential(2,0,X$Gap#31.117895/169.573210))
IntUnif   VARIABLE (Uniform(2,0,2#X$Gap))
TraceSeq  FUNCTION P2,D300

* One source transaction creates arrivals by SPLIT.
          GENERATE 0,0,0,1
          ASSIGN 2,1
NextGap   TEST E X$Flow,4,NotTrace
          ADVANCE (FN$TraceSeq#X$Gap/169.573210)
          ASSIGN 2+,1
          TEST LE P2,300,Wrap
          TRANSFER ,SendJob
Wrap      ASSIGN 2,1
          TRANSFER ,SendJob
NotTrace  TEST E X$Flow,1,TryHypo
          ADVANCE V$IntExp
          TRANSFER ,SendJob
TryHypo   TEST E X$Flow,2,UseUniform
          ADVANCE V$IntHypo
          TRANSFER ,SendJob
UseUniform ADVANCE V$IntUnif
SendJob   SPLIT 1,Arrival
          TRANSFER ,NextGap
Arrival   MARK
          MARK 1
          TRANSFER .4,,LaneTwo
          GATE NU ServerOne,DropJob
          SEIZE ServerOne
          ASSIGN 4,1
          TRANSFER ,BeginSvc
LaneTwo   GATE SNF BufferTwo,DropJob
          ENTER BufferTwo
          QUEUE LineTwo
          SEIZE ServerTwo
          DEPART LineTwo
          ASSIGN 4,2
BeginSvc  TABULATE WaitSmo
          TEST E X$Mode,1,TryErl
          ADVANCE (Exponential(3,0,X$MeanS))
          TRANSFER ,FinishSvc
TryErl    TEST E X$Mode,2,TryHyp
          ADVANCE (Exponential(3,0,X$MeanS/2)+Exponential(3,0,X$MeanS/2))
          TRANSFER ,FinishSvc
TryHyp    TRANSFER 0.112701665,Fast,Slow
Fast      ADVANCE (Exponential(3,0,X$MeanS/1.774596669))
          TRANSFER ,FinishSvc
Slow      ADVANCE (Exponential(3,0,X$MeanS/0.225403331))
FinishSvc TABULATE SojSmo
          TEST E P4,1,FreeTwo
          RELEASE ServerOne
          TRANSFER ,CountEnd
FreeTwo   RELEASE ServerTwo
          LEAVE BufferTwo
          TRANSFER ,CountEnd
DropJob   SAVEVALUE Lost+,1
          TRANSFER ,CountEnd
CountEnd  SAVEVALUE Done+,1
          TEST GE X$Done,X$Limit,GoAway
          TRANSFER ,OutFile
GoAway    TERMINATE 0
OutFile   OPEN (PolyCatenate("C:/Users/Vasy/LEARNING/ITMO/ITMO-study-labs/Modelling/lab-3/gpss/results/",String(X$Case),"_",String(X$Stage),".txt")),1
          WRITE (Catenate("case=",String(X$Case))),1
          WRITE (Catenate("stage=",String(X$Stage))),1
          WRITE (Catenate("clock=",String(AC1))),1
          WRITE (Catenate("elapsed=",String(C1))),1
          WRITE (Catenate("lost=",String(X$Lost))),1
          WRITE (Catenate("done=",String(X$Done))),1
          WRITE (Catenate("w=",String(TB$WaitSmo))),1
          WRITE (Catenate("t=",String(TB$SojSmo))),1
          WRITE (Catenate("sdw=",String(TD$WaitSmo))),1
          WRITE (Catenate("sdt=",String(TD$SojSmo))),1
          WRITE (Catenate("rho1=",String(FR$ServerOne/1000))),1
          WRITE (Catenate("rho2=",String(FR$ServerTwo/1000))),1
          WRITE (Catenate("lq2=",String(QA$LineTwo))),1
          WRITE (Catenate("served=",String(TC$SojSmo))),1
          CLOSE 99,1,IOError
          TEST E P99,0,IOError
          TERMINATE 1
IOError   TERMINATE 1
